# Skills · U · part 30

823 entries, umap-dimensionality-reduction to umwelt-owi-frist-pruefen. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **umap-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have high-dimensional per-transcript feature vectors (e.g., codon-, GC-, sequence-, peptide-, or BERT-encoded ORF-attention features) and need a 2D scatter visualization to check whether lncRNAs and PCTs form separable clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-dimensionality-reduction--01617154f54bc7d6))
- **umap-dimensionality-reduction-embedding**: Use when a single-cell (scRNA-seq or scATAC-seq) count matrix has been normalized and feature-selected, and you need a 2D embedding for cluster visualization, annotation, or Monocle 3 trajectory topology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-dimensionality-reduction-embedding))
- **umap-dimensionality-reduction-of-msi-pixel-spectra**: Use when raw imzML MALDI MSI pixel spectra need unsupervised grouping by spectral similarity — e.g. for tissue segmentation, matrix/off-tissue pixel detection, or resolving molecular heterogeneity of a cell population — and no cluster number is known a priori. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/umap-dimensionality-reduction-of-msi-pixel-spectra))
- **umap-dimensionality-reduction-spatial-proteomics**: Use when a CODEX or other spatial proteomics cell-by-protein expression matrix (e.g., AnnData) must be embedded for downstream Leiden clustering and spatial tile visualization, without prior normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-dimensionality-reduction-spatial-proteomics))
- **umap-dimensionality-reduction-starter**: Use this skill to embed a tiny labeled feature matrix with UMAP and summarize the resulting 2D geometry. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/statistical-and-machine-learning-foundations-for-science/umap-dimensionality-reduction-starter) · ★46)
- **umap-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when a trained VAE has produced a low-dimensional latent variable (e.g. 5-dim Code layer output) for tens of thousands of MALDI FT-ICR MSI spectra and you need a 2D embedding to visually assess normal/tumor class separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/umap-dimensionality-reduction-visualization))
- **umap-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when annotating cells at a later timepoint (t1) against known labels from an initial timepoint (t0) and you need a 2D UMAP projection to inspect whether novel-cell calls or annotation assignments form coherent clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-dimensionality-reduction-visualization--e0720e433b027faf))
- **umap-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when running SCASL on single-cell SMART-seq junction/AS matrices and you need a 2D visualization of spectral-clustering results on the imputed AS probability matrix (dimension_reduction_method='umap' in configs/srr.yaml). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-dimensionality-reduction-visualization--60d789245cad8064))
- **umap-embedding-and-signature-score-visualization**: Use when per-cell signature scores have been computed (e.g., UCell scores stored in altExp(sce,'UCell') or Seurat metadata) and you need to visualize signature enrichment across cells in 2D to interpret clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-and-signature-score-visualization))
- **umap-embedding-computation-seurat**: Use when single-cell multi-omics data lack true spatial coordinates but a spatially aware model (e.g., SMOPCA) requires a 2D location input per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/umap-embedding-computation-seurat))
- **umap-embedding-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking how well cell-cell communication features (UMAP embeddings or tensor factor loadings) separate phenotypic contexts such as COVID-19 status and severity, using a supervised classifier's cross-validated AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-evaluation))
- **umap-embedding-mixing-assessment**: Use when you have cell-by-gene expression matrices (e.g. expr_mat.csv) segmented independently from two replicate spatial transcriptomics sections and need to quantify whether segmentation output is replicable across replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-mixing-assessment))
- **umap-embedding-of-layer-representations**: Use when a trained deep learning model (e.g. a protein-sequence CNN built with autoBioSeqpy) needs its hidden-layer outputs visualized in 2-D UMAP space for layer-by-layer inspection, diagnosis or refinement of representations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-of-layer-representations))
- **umap-embedding-of-metabolic-profiles**: Use when you have a per-cell normalized metabolic profile matrix (e.g. SpaceM's MORPHnMOL.csv with ~30k cells) and need a 2D visualization of resolved metabolic states after graph-based clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/umap-embedding-of-metabolic-profiles))
- **umap-embedding-of-ms2-fingerprint-matrices**: Use when you have an aligned MEMO matrix of MS2 fingerprints (e.g. 1,600 plant extracts) and need a 2D ordination to check whether biologically active samples co-cluster or appear as singletons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/umap-embedding-of-ms2-fingerprint-matrices))
- **umap-embedding-of-omics-matrices**: Use when a high-dimensional omics abundance matrix (thousands of metabolite features x tens-to-hundreds of isolates) needs a low-dimensional embedding to test whether samples cluster by a known grouping such as bacterial taxonomy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/umap-embedding-of-omics-matrices))
- **umap-embedding-of-predicted-expression-profiles**: Use when RNAForecaster (or another gene-dimensional forecaster) has produced future/perturbed expression profiles and you need to verify they occupy biologically plausible expression space by checking co-clustering with real or simulated post-perturbation cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-of-predicted-expression-profiles))
- **umap-embedding-of-single-cell-data**: Use when two or more a priori class labels capture distinct biological sources of variance in single-cell data and you need one unsupervised embedding that preserves all of them simultaneously. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-of-single-cell-data))
- **umap-embedding-overlay-of-apa-scores**: Use when single-cell poly(A) site data (PACdataset) has computed per-cell APA indices like RUD and you need to check spatial coherence of those scores on the cell embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-overlay-of-apa-scores))
- **umap-embedding-overlay-visualization**: Use when per-cell scores (DA-seq differential abundance scores, cluster or severity annotations) need to be visualized in low-dimensional space on a UMAP built from scMerge2-adjusted single-cell expression data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-overlay-visualization))
- **umap-embedding-parameter-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether cell-type annotations from an embedding-dependent annotation method (e.g. Census, whose label-stabilizing step uses UMAP clusters and prediction contours) remain stable across UMAP hyperparameter variations like n.neighbors and min.dist. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-parameter-sensitivity-analysis))
- **umap-embedding-projection-and-distance-computation**: Use when benchmarking UMI deduplication or read-level count-recovery tools against a ground-truth gene-by-cell UMI matrix and you need a cell-position-level accuracy metric (mean Euclidean UMAP distance per tool) rather than only correlation statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umap-embedding-projection-and-distance-computation))

Normen: § 1684 BGB (Umgang Eltern), § 1685 BGB (Umgang Dritte), § 1697a BGB (Kindeswohl-Massstab), § 89 FamFG (Vollstreckung Ordnungsmittel), § 165 FamFG (Vermittlungsverfahren). Prüfraster: Wochenend-/Ferien-/Feiertagsregelungen, Wechselmodell vs. Residenzmodell, Begleitumgang, Vollstreckbarkeit. Output Umgangsregelungs-Mustervorlagen. - **umi-and-barcode-extraction-from-nanopore-reads**: Use when raw 10x-structured Oxford nanopore FASTQ must be demultiplexed by cell barcode and UMI before read alignment or isoform/gene reconstruction, and no matching short-read data is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/umi-and-barcode-extraction-from-nanopore-reads))
- **umi-aware-deduplication**: Decision support for choosing between SAMtools markdup and UMI-tools dedup when aligning cfMeDIP-seq libraries that may or may not incorporate UMI barcodes, as declared in the YAML configuration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/umi-aware-deduplication))
- **umi-barcode-extraction-regex-matching**: Use when input FASTQ libraries (single-end or paired-end cfMeDIP-seq) carry UMI barcodes for sequence error suppression and barcodes must be extracted and reads lacking the expected barcode filtered before adapter trimming. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/umi-barcode-extraction-regex-matching))
- **umi-based-misalignment-correction**: Use when Nanopore long reads aligned with minimap2 yield spurious isoforms from wrongly mapped small internal exons (e.g., VPS28-201) or truncated ends, and read-level counts must be corrected within UMI clusters before isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-based-misalignment-correction))
- **umi-based-read-grouping**: Use when you have a barcode-, UMI- and gene-tagged Nanopore BAM (with BC, U8, GE tags) and need one consensus cDNA per cell-UMI molecule for downstream isoform or variant analysis (Sicelore Step 4.b). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/umi-based-read-grouping))
- **umi-based-variant-quality-filtering**: Use when calling SNVs from UMI-based scRNA-seq data (e.g. Cell Ranger-aligned 10x droplet data) to classify individual cells by genotype, quantify chimerism or multiplets, or need variant quality criteria tied to UMI/cell-barcode support rather than read depth alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-based-variant-quality-filtering))
- **umi-cell-barcode-consensus-generation**: Use when single-cell CRISPR screen gRNA library BAM alignments must be deduplicated into per-UMI per-cell consensus gRNA sequences before variant calling, e.g. to reproduce the 28.4% mutant molecule fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-cell-barcode-consensus-generation))
- **umi-cell-barcode-gene-combination-deduplication**: Use when droplet-based 10x Genomics libraries from multiple samples were sequenced together on patterned flow-cell instruments (HiSeq 4000, HiSeq X, NovaSeq) and swapped molecules sharing UMI, cell barcode, and gene across samples must be removed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-cell-barcode-gene-combination-deduplication))
- **umi-cell-barcode-synthesis**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq UMI deduplication or read-level tools on 10X-style data and you need synthetic reads whose true UMI counts and cell barcodes are known (e.g. from a 10X BAM with CB/UMI structure). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-cell-barcode-synthesis))
- **umi-cellbarcode-gene-collision-resolution**: Use when processing molecule information files from multiplexed 10x Genomics droplet libraries sequenced on patterned flow-cell instruments (HiSeq 4000, HiSeq X, NovaSeq) where ~2.5% of reads may be mislabelled between samples via barcode swapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-cellbarcode-gene-collision-resolution))
- **umi-cluster-size-scatter-reduction**: Use when quantifying isoforms from single-cell or spatially barcoded Nanopore long reads with high per-base error (85-99%), where UMI scattering inflates UMI counts and fixed-edit-distance collapsing would overestimate expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-cluster-size-scatter-reduction))
- **umi-consensus-read-mapping**: Use when raw Nanopore long reads from a 10x Genomics droplet scRNA-seq library must be converted into per-cell gene/isoform UMI count matrices via consensus sequences and strict Gencode isoform assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/umi-consensus-read-mapping))
- **umi-consensus-sequence-construction**: Use when processing a single-cell CRISPR screen gRNA library BAM (mapped to the designed gRNA cassette reference) whose reads carry cell barcodes and UMIs, and you need per-UMI consensus sequences before calling mutations or assigning gRNAs to cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-consensus-sequence-construction))
- **umi-count-aggregation-per-phase**: Use when phase-annotated h5ad data (adata.obs['cell_cycle_theta'] from a DeepCycle run) is available and you need to verify that UMI counts per cell increase roughly 2-fold along the cell cycle prior to the mitotic drop. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-count-aggregation-per-phase))
- **umi-count-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have long-read single-cell (10x ONT) count matrices and a matching short-read Cell Ranger run of the same library and want to quantify cell barcode detection and quantification agreement via correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/umi-count-correlation-analysis))
- **umi-count-inflation-benchmark-analysis**: Use when recovering true UMIs from error-prone Nanopore single-cell long reads, or when testing whether a UMI collapsing strategy (e.g. DBSCAN at eps=2) inflates UMI counts at high PCR amplification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-count-inflation-benchmark-analysis))
- **umi-count-matrix-construction-from-bam**: Use when you have an scRNA-seq BAM file aligned to mm10, a gene annotation GTF (e.g., gencode.vM10.annotation.gtf), and a chromosome size file, and you need gene- and inter-gene-by-cell UMI count matrices as ground truth for simulation or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-count-matrix-construction-from-bam))
- **umi-count-matrix-integration-10x**: Use when you have a cellranger count output folder for a 10x scRNAseq sample (e.g. HIV donor or SARS-CoV-2 airway data) and need pathogen UMI counts merged into features.tsv.gz/matrix.mtx.gz so Seurat or standard single-cell pipelines can analyze infected cells downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-count-matrix-integration-10x))
- **umi-count-matrix-processing**: Use when the input is a raw single-cell UMI count matrix and you need zero-inflation- and over-dispersion-aware low-dimensional signal extraction without a preliminary normalization step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-count-matrix-processing))
- **umi-counting-and-error-correction**: Use when processing droplet-based scRNA-seq FASTQ data with cell barcodes and UMIs (e.g. 10x GemCode/Chromium), to deduplicate reads into molecule counts or to quantify conversion efficiency against known ERCC spike-in inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-counting-and-error-correction))
- **umi-deduplication-benchmarking**: Use when synthetic scRNA-seq reads with known ground-truth UMI counts are available and you must rank UMI deduplication tools by accuracy and runtime, reproducing the scReadSim Nature Communications 2023 benchmark (Fig. 4a-d). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-deduplication-benchmarking))
- **umi-dependent-gamma-parameter-regression**: Use when constructing a cell type-specific expression prior for scPower power analysis from pilot scRNA-seq UMI count matrices, requiring gene-mean distributions parameterized by sequencing depth (mean UMI per cell). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/umi-dependent-gamma-parameter-regression))
- **umi-duplicate-collapsing**: Use when counting molecules from UMI-tagged RNA-Seq alignments: a sorted SAM file exists and duplicate UMI at identical genomic positions must be merged into single observations before per-gene counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-duplicate-collapsing))
- **umi-error-corrected-consensus-analysis**: Use when ScNaUmi-seq 10x-linked Nanopore reads are available and you need UMI-guided error-corrected consensus molecules to call site-level sequence variation (SNPs/RNA editing) per molecule or per cell at defined genomic positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/umi-error-corrected-consensus-analysis))
- **umi-error-correction-and-collapsing**: Use when processing single-cell amplicon sequencing data for expressed lineage barcodes and you must remove PCR chimeras and collapse UMI sequencing errors before per-cell barcode annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-error-correction-and-collapsing))
- **umi-extraction-and-pcr-duplicate-removal**: Use when processing paired-end FASTQ from spatially barcoded ST data (e.g., MOB ST, SRA: SRR3382373) to attach barcode/UMI identifiers to read 2 and deduplicate PCR artifacts before poly(A) site identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-extraction-and-pcr-duplicate-removal))
- **umi-extraction-from-single-cell-cdna-reads**: Use when processing long reads from 10x Genomics 5'-anchored whole-transcriptome cDNA libraries and per-read cell-barcode and UMI tags are needed before downstream mutation or isoform calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/umi-extraction-from-single-cell-cdna-reads))
- **umi-extraction-into-read-names**: Use when processing sci-L3 FASTQ reads that must be labeled with their SSS barcode, IVT UMI, and three-level combinatorial barcode (Tn5/RT and ligation) before alignment or single-cell demultiplexing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/umi-extraction-into-read-names))

- **umi-threshold-barcode-set-partition**: Use when starting from a raw unfiltered count matrix (mtx or h5) and you need to define PU-learning training sets for distinguishing cell-free from cell-containing droplets by total UMI count. - **umi-threshold-based-cell-filtering**: Use when you have raw gene-by-cell spliced/unspliced UMI count matrices from scKB (kallisto/bustools) and need to remove low-quality or empty droplets non-arbitrarily before downstream Seurat or RNA-velocity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/umi-threshold-based-cell-filtering))
- **umi-validity-filtering-and-hamming-correction**: Use when processing droplet scRNA-seq FASTQ reads (e.g. 10x Genomics GemCode/Chromium data) into a UMI count matrix and you need to remove invalid UMIs and collapse sequencing-error UMIs to avoid inflating molecule counts. - **umi-vs-non-umi-protocol-zero-inflation-analysis**: Use when validating that a Bayesian scRNA-seq count model reproduces observed batch-level zero rates, or when assessing whether an explicit zero-inflated dropout component is justified versus a plain negative-binomial fit. - **umuo-workstyle**: 按 umuo 的个人偏好处理软件开发、项目调研、技术学习和文档任务（涉及编码、配置、设计或文档时均应先读取本技能）：用轻量 SDD 明确需求与验收，新需求先做开源方案 spike，新项目说明技术选型理由与取舍，优先复用成熟实现；强制执行个人代码规范（默认 4 空格缩进、if 等控制语句必带花括号、类与接口使用多行中文 Javadoc、禁止通配符导入、XML 父子嵌套标签分行缩进），重视可用交付、原理、交互与隐私。补充专项技能，不替代当前任务与项目规则。 ([umuo/skills](https://github.com/umuo/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/umuo-workstyle))

- **umwelt-login**: Use when entering the Umwelt world at the start of a session (or after a context reset while living in the world). Login here is NOT a server handshake — it is connecting your avatar's senses to your cognition: a proactive half (orient — where you are, what's here, what's waiting) and a reactive half (establish or verify the runtime's wake path so an addressed crossing reaches cognition). Claude arms a session-scoped Monitor; Codex verifies its persistent managed bridge. Load it once per session in the world.
